Second transcription's note: Counts taxpayer/spouse person entries plus dependents claimed; the supplied interface has no separate flag excluding only a taxpayer or spouse who can be claimed as another person’s dependent, so sa.dependent refuses the line when it applies.

Ruling: interface gap — the candidate's line has no shown_when, so where the project hides it (no Form 1095-A, Form 8962 not completed, or the annual or monthly route not taken: i8962 p. 15, "If you checked “Yes” on line 10 and you are completing line 11, do not complete lines 12 through 23."; i8962 p. 16, "If you checked “No” on line 10 and you are completing lines 12 through 23, do not complete line 11.") it shows an amount or needs answers the hidden line never asks; the request labels shown_when Optional (T-163), as ruled in earlier rounds; the expression is the project's. Triage executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/f8962.deepseek.json`, rerun 2026-11-03: 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records from 40 bases): 222 of 2,123 comparisons agree; differences by kind {"candidate-refuses": 137, "candidate-shows-skipped": 1606, "candidate-unknown": 26, "project-unknown": 132}; on completed returns {"candidate-shows-skipped": 337}. The refusal-path differences are on returns the project or the candidate refuses: project-refuses where the project refuses at its own cited gates (f8962.alloc, f8962.marriage, f8962.void_form, f8962.premium_adjust, f8962.alien_branch, f8962.two_counted, dependent screens) or by a refusal reaching the line from another form, and candidate-refuses on the candidate's own declarations (for example sa.excluded_income and sa.dependent, read on records where the project hides the line); none is a completed-return amount. With the witness cap lifted (scratch), all 337 completed-return differences were listed: 337 are on records where the project hides the line; 0 where the project shows the line. No completed-return value difference.

Second transcription's note: Form 2555 lines 45 and 50 are not in the supplied interface; returns with Form 2555, Form 4563 or Puerto Rico exclusion are refused through sa.excluded_income.

Ruling: model error and interface gap — the candidate adds f1040.tax_exempt_interest where the source enters Form 1040 line 2a (i8962 p. 8, Worksheet 1-1, line 2, "Enter any tax-exempt interest from Form 1040, 1040-SR, or 1040-NR, line 2a"); f1040.line2a is f1040.tax_exempt_interest plus exempt-interest dividends (div.box12; forms/2025/f1040.yaml, citing i1040gi p. 25, "Also include on line 2a any exempt-interest dividends from a mutual fund or other regulated investment company"), so the candidate understates modified AGI when Form 1099-DIV box 12 is nonzero; no sampled return with a Form 1095-A had box 12. Visibility: the candidate's line has no shown_when, so where the project hides it (no Form 1095-A, Form 8962 not completed, or the annual or monthly route not taken: i8962 p. 15, "If you checked “Yes” on line 10 and you are completing line 11, do not complete lines 12 through 23."; i8962 p. 16, "If you checked “No” on line 10 and you are completing lines 12 through 23, do not complete line 11.") it shows an amount or needs answers the hidden line never asks; the request labels shown_when Optional (T-163), as ruled in earlier rounds. Ruling: model error and scope — the candidate sums dependent.ptc_magi_counted over every dependent roster entry, not only the dependents claimed on the return (i8962 p. 8, Line 2b, "Enter on line 2b the combined modified AGI for your dependents who are required to file an income tax return because their income meets the income tax return filing threshold."); on stage18.mut.unclaimed_relative (incomplete return) it counts an unclaimed relative's $30,000 (project $8,000, candidate $38,000), which reaches lines 3, 5, 7, 8a, 8b, 11(c) to 11(e), 24 and 26 there; without the dependent filter it also computes where the project refuses at the dependent screens (dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.claimed_no_tin, dependent.qc_tiebreak and others) on returns those screens refuse. Every completed-return difference is an unknown on a record where the project hides the line. Triage executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/f8962.deepseek.json`, rerun 2026-11-03: 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records from 40 bases): 1,601 of 2,123 comparisons agree; differences by kind {"candidate-unknown": 372, "project-refuses": 23, "project-unknown": 126, "value": 1}; on completed returns {"candidate-unknown": 51}. The refusal-path differences are on returns the project or the candidate refuses: project-refuses where the project refuses at its own cited gates (f8962.alloc, f8962.marriage, f8962.void_form, f8962.premium_adjust, f8962.alien_branch, f8962.two_counted, dependent screens) or by a refusal reaching the line from another form, and candidate-refuses on the candidate's own declarations (for example sa.excluded_income and sa.dependent, read on records where the project hides the line); none is a completed-return amount. With the witness cap lifted (scratch), all 51 completed-return differences were listed: 51 are on records where the project hides the line (candidate-unknown there needs {"dependent[a].ptc_files": 1, "dependent[child].ptc_files": 1, "dependent[dep_a].ptc_files": 49}, answers the interview does not ask when the line is not reached); 0 where the project shows the line. No completed-return value difference.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: f8962.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Tax family size. Enter your tax family size. See instructions."
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.h_persons_count, f8962.h_dependents_count]}
    refuses: [sa.dependent]
    note: "Counts taxpayer/spouse person entries plus dependents claimed; the supplied interface has no separate flag excluding only a taxpayer or spouse who can be claimed as another person’s dependent, so sa.dependent refuses the line when it applies."
  - id: f8962.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Modified AGI. Enter your modified AGI. See instructions."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line11a, f1040.tax_exempt_interest, f8962.h_ss_nontaxable]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income]
    note: "Form 2555 lines 45 and 50 are not in the supplied interface; returns with Form 2555, Form 4563 or Puerto Rico exclusion are refused through sa.excluded_income."
  - id: f8962.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Enter the total of your dependents’ modified AGI. See instructions."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.ptc_magi_counted}
  - id: f8962.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Household income. Add the amounts on lines 2a and 2b. See instructions."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.h_line3_raw, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: f8962.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Federal poverty line. Enter the federal poverty line amount from Table 1-1, 1-2, or 1-3. See instructions."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: ptc_poverty_line, keys: [f8962.fpl_table, f8962.line1]}
  - id: f8962.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Household income as a percentage of federal poverty line (see instructions)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: f8962.line3, whole: f8962.line4, cap: 400}
  - id: f8962.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "Applicable figure. Using your line 5 percentage, locate your applicable figure on the table in the instructions."
    type: Rate
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: ptc_applicable_figure, keys: [f8962.line5]}
  - id: f8962.line8a
    printed: "8a"
    label: "Annual contribution amount. Multiply line 3 by line 7. Round to nearest whole dollar amount."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8962.line3, rate: f8962.line7, unit: 100}
  - id: f8962.line8b
    printed: "8b"
    label: "Monthly contribution amount. Divide line 8a by 12. Round to nearest whole dollar amount."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8962.line8a, rate: {param: ptc.twelfth}, unit: 100}
  - id: f8962.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Are you allocating policy amounts with another taxpayer or do you want to use the alternative calculation for year of marriage? See instructions."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.alloc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.married_in_2025, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "See the instructions to determine if you can use line 11 or must complete lines 12 through 23."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.any, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_all_uniform, {const: true}]}, {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.premium_adjust, {const: true}]}}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_line9_no
  - id: f8962.line11a
    printed: "11(a)"
    label: "Annual Totals — (a) Annual enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.line33a}
    refuses: [f8962.other]
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_line11
    note: "The supplied Form 1095-A premium inputs are treated as already net of any premiums refunded in 2025; f8962.premium_adjust is used to select the monthly calculation when coverage began after the first day of a month."
  - id: f8962.line11b
    printed: "11(b)"
    label: "Annual Totals — (b) Annual applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.line33b, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}}
    refuses:
      - {when: f8962.same_state_counted, when_known: true}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_line11
  - id: f8962.line11c
    printed: "11(c)"
    label: "Annual Totals — (c) Annual contribution amount (line 8a)"

    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8a}

  - id: f8962.line11d

    type: NonNegDollars

    printed: "23(f)"
    label: "December — (f) Monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21–32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_dec}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly
  - id: f8962.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Total PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (e), or add lines 12 through 23, column (e), and enter the total here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_not_applicable_below, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_not_applicable_below, {const: true}]}}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}]}, then: f8962.line11e}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_not_applicable_below, {const: true}]}}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}]}, then: f8962.h_monthly_e_sum}]}
  - id: f8962.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Advance payment of PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (f), or add lines 12 through 23, column (f), and enter the total here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.line11f}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_monthly_f_sum}]}
  - id: f8962.line26
    printed: "26"
    label: "Net PTC. If line 24 is greater than line 25, subtract line 25 from line 24. Enter the difference here and on Schedule 3 (Form 1040), line 9. If line 24 equals line 25, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line24, take: f8962.line25}
  - id: f8962.line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Excess advance payment of PTC. If line 25 is greater than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 25. Enter the difference here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line25, take: f8962.line24}
  - id: f8962.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Repayment limitation (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 200, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_200}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 200, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 300, type: Count}]}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_300}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 300, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_400}, {guard: {op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    note: "Line 28 is blank when line 5 is 400 or more; that blank is represented as zero here and line 29 ignores it in that case."
  - id: f8962.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Excess advance PTC repayment. Enter the smaller of line 27 or line 28 here and on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), line 1a."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.line27}, {guard: {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.h_smaller_27_28}]}
  - id: f8962.forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 1095-A"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.one}
  - id: f8962.any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any Form 1095-A"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8962.forms, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: f8962.h_ss_nontaxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nontaxable social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line6a, take: f1040.line6b}
  - id: f8962.h_line3_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 before zero floor"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.line2a, f8962.line2b]}
  - id: f8962.h_persons_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxpayer and spouse entries"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.one}
  - id: f8962.h_dependents_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependents claimed"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}}
  - id: f8962.h_uniform_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 1095-A with uniform 12-month amounts"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.uniform, {const: true}]}}
  - id: f8962.h_all_uniform
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "All Forms 1095-A uniform"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_uniform_count, f8962.forms]}
  - id: f8962.h_line9_no
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 9 is no"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line9, {const: true}]}}
  - id: f8962.h_show_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Show line 11"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_line9_no, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: f8962.h_show_monthly
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Show monthly lines"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_line9_no, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: f8962.h_monthly_e_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Sum of monthly PTC allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.line12e, f8962.line13e, f8962.line14e, f8962.line15e, f8962.line16e, f8962.line17e, f8962.line18e, f8962.line19e, f8962.line20e, f8962.line21e, f8962.line22e, f8962.line23e]}
  - id: f8962.h_monthly_f_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Sum of monthly advance payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.line12f, f8962.line13f, f8962.line14f, f8962.line15f, f8962.line16f, f8962.line17f, f8962.line18f, f8962.line19f, f8962.line20f, f8962.line21f, f8962.line22f, f8962.line23f]}
  - id: f8962.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: f8962.h_aptc_paid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "APTC paid"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8962.line25, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f8962.h_below_100
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Household income below 100% FPL"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 100, type: Count}]}
  - id: f8962.h_estimate_exception
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Estimated income at least 100% exception"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.estimate_100, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_aptc_paid, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: f8962.h_alien_exception
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Alien lawfully present exception"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f8962.alien_below, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.h_below_exception
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Below 100% FPL exception"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_estimate_exception, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_alien_exception, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: f8962.h_not_applicable_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Not an applicable taxpayer because below 100% FPL without exception"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_below_100, {const: true}]}, {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_below_exception, {const: true}]}}]}
  - id: f8962.h_repay_200
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Repayment limitation below 200%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_200}
  - id: f8962.h_repay_300
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Repayment limitation at least 200% but less than 300%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_300}
  - id: f8962.h_repay_400
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Repayment limitation at least 300% but less than 400%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_400}
  - id: f8962.h_smaller_27_28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 27 or line 28"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line27, f8962.line28]}
```
